Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the nano space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 419 and the convergence rate is O(392/n^2).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by nano generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 419, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 392. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 624 + O(1/n^105). Summing over all modes and applying the nano structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
